*Commander Anthology Volume II*, like its older sibling *Commander Anthology*, is a collection of four previously released *Commander* decks from years past. This year, though, there's a slight twist.

The legends got the new frame treatment.

Let's look at what that means. First, the decks. The four decks coming back are "Devour for Power" (helmed by [The Mimeoplasm](https://web.archive.org/web/20180508000000/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=The+Mimeoplasm)), "Built from Scratch" (the [Daretti, Scrap Savant](https://web.archive.org/web/20180508000000/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Daretti%2C+Scrap+Savant) deck that I turned into one of my most complicated and broken Commander decks), "Wade into Battle" (with [Kalemne, Disciple of Iroas](https://web.archive.org/web/20180508000000/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kalemne%2C+Disciple+of+Iroas) leading the charge), and "Breed Lethality" (with everyone's favorite proliferator, [Atraxa, Praetors' Voice](https://web.archive.org/web/20180508000000/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Atraxa%2C+Praetors%27+Voice)).

All of these will be available when *Commander Anthology Vol. II* releases June 8.

Now let's look at the legends getting the new frame treatment for the first time. The following got the new frame *and* are in foil:

![The Mimeoplasm](https://media.wizards.com/2018/cm2/en_1xXTZa3rrs.png)![Damia, Sage of Stone](https://media.wizards.com/2018/cm2/en_bfQIly1vjS.png)![Vorosh, the Hunter](https://media.wizards.com/2018/cm2/en_Mle6uJxQbx.png)

![Bosh, Iron Golem](https://media.wizards.com/2018/cm2/en_9tnHPWB4oZ.png)![Feldon of the Third Path](https://media.wizards.com/2018/cm2/en_aOuXKOo0e0.png)

![Kalemne, Disciple of Iroas](https://media.wizards.com/2018/cm2/en_HGAd9TFkzw.png)![Anya, Merciless Angel](https://media.wizards.com/2018/cm2/en_kPgSgGv8xw.png)![Gisela, Blade of Goldnight](https://media.wizards.com/2018/cm2/en_VPLhx2vT5s.png)

![Atraxa, Praetors' Voice](https://media.wizards.com/2018/cm2/en_WUAgjMgyqw.png)![Ikra Shidiqi, the Usurper](https://media.wizards.com/2018/cm2/en_piDeSO2zaO.png)
